# Field Registry Missing / Placeholder Description List

Phase: FR-DQ-0A Missing Field Description Export

This is a read-only export of Field Registry entries whose `description` is missing or appears to be placeholder / template text. It does not add official descriptions and does not modify Field Registry JSON.

## Scope

| Scope | Path | Status |
|---|---|---|
| BuiltIn | `RA2IniEditor.Infrastructure/FieldRegistry/BuiltIn/builtin-yr-ares-phobos-fallback-v3.2.fields.json` | Scanned |
| Global active | `C:\Users\PC\AppData\Roaming\RA2IniEditor\FieldRegistry\active` | Scanned when files existed |
| Project active | `C:\Users\PC\Desktop\RA2Ini_IDE\.ra2inieditor\field-registry\active` | Directory missing |

## Detection Patterns

- `null / empty / whitespace`
- `原始英文说明`
- `不直接用于 Hover`
- `不能直接用于 Hover`
- `placeholder`
- `TODO`
- `TBD`
- `待补充`
- `未整理`
- `模板`
- `占位`

## Priority Rules

- `P0`: high-frequency gameplay / draft fields named in the FR-DQ-0A request.
- `P1`: explicit BuiltIn placeholder wording such as `原始英文说明` or `不直接用于 Hover`.
- `P2`: missing / empty description.
- `P3`: other placeholder-like terms such as `placeholder`, `TODO`, `TBD`, `模板`, or `占位`.
